# Third-party notices

annodiff itself is MIT licensed; dependencies retain their own licenses.

Generated by `python3 licenses/generate.py` from `Cargo.lock`, crate license files, and the reviewed upstream material in `licenses/supplemental.json`. Includes optional, build, and target-specific dependencies; not all are linked into every binary.

The selected license column records our choice where alternatives are offered. Upstream texts may mention other available licenses; preserving those texts does not select them. Additional Unicode terms and bundled-asset notices are retained.

Include this file and `LICENSE` with binary distributions. Both are also embedded in the executable and available through `annodiff --licenses`.
